! Title: No-Bullshit uBlock Origin Supplement
! Description: Additive paywall, promotion, popup, survey, newsletter, and YouTube-nag cleanup.
! Version: 2026.08.22.1
! Last modified: 22 August 2026
! Expires: 1 day (update frequency)
! Syntax: uBlock Origin static filters
! License: GPL-3.0-or-later; https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html
! Baseline excluded: yokoffing/filterlists main @ 80d1341d220a773424a4644a2b52eb62a94b1b8f
! Baseline URL: https://github.com/yokoffing/filterlists
! Upstream attribution: The EasyList authors (https://easylist.to/) and magnolia1234/BPC.
! Generated: exact lines, normalized network equivalents, and overlapping cosmetic targets were removed.
! Active rules in this supplement: 10063
!
! IMPORTANT:
! - Intended for full uBlock Origin. Advanced scriptlets will not all work in uBO Lite or other blockers.
! - Keep uBlock's built-in filters and "uBlock filters – Quick fixes" enabled for live YouTube ad fixes.
! - Do not also enable the full BPC, EasyList Annoyances, or EasyList Newsletter lists: those are upstream inputs.
! - Aggressive by design: newsletter forms, surveys, chat, social widgets, and some login/paywall state may disappear.
! - A filter can remove a client-side wall; it cannot restore article text the server never delivered.
